Cross-sectional, contrarian. Compares each buyable stock's N-day return against the pool's median return; the worst k laggards (those falling at least min_underperformance_percent below the median) get a small buy. Pairs with trailing_stop — profit_take captures gains as they happen on the way up; trailing_stop captures the residual position after a real reversal.
